([jesperhagg/pm-operating-system](https://github.com/jesperhagg/pm-operating-system/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/agent-playbook-update))
- **agent-playbooks**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/bash/agent-playbooks) · ★745)
- **agent-playtest**: Organizzare playtest di un serious game con sotto-agenti che interpretano personas (studente, adulto curioso, ottimizzatore, esperto di UX), via browser headless o su screenshot, senza rubare il focus all'utente; e raccogliere feedback di giocatori umani legati alla build. Usare quando l'utente chiede un playtest, «è giocabile?», un giro con Haiku/Sonnet, personas, o un report con immagini. ([CIMAFoundation/isola-di-roccavento](https://github.com/CIMAFoundation/isola-di-roccavento/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/agent-playtest))
- **agent-playtest**: 找 3 個從沒看過平台的 agent 實際打一局，判斷參賽體驗「能用」又「好用」，並寫試玩報告。ticket 標了 playtest、改了 skill.md 或錯誤訊息、或要確認參賽 agent 用起來順不順時使用。 ([LiaoAnn/agent-casino](https://github.com/LiaoAnn/agent-casino/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/agent-playtest))
- **agent-playtesting**: Playtest by driving the live game: deterministic launch, frame-stamped probes, scripted or recorded input. ([SummerEngine/summer](https://github.com/SummerEngine/summer/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/skills/agent-playtesting) · ★109 · 4 copies)
- **agent-playwright-session-failed-mid-au-44d9de26b997794f**: Fixes agents whose Playwright crawl of an authorized web app dies halfway, losing the session and the crawl frontier. Use when a crawl starts fine and then fails with session errors, leaving no usable crawl state. Only for web apps you are engaged to test. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-playwright-session-failed-mid-au-44d9de26b997794f))
- **agent-plaza**: Agent Plaza selfie delivery, optional Turing Falls steering, and follow-up guidance for AgentVillage Hermes installs. Sends local selfie images through Telegram, stores only ops telemetry/state/media, and helps route later chat replies toward IRL closeout without deterministic parsing. ([Edge-City/agentvillage](https://github.com/Edge-City/agentvillage/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-plaza) · ★21 · 2 copies)
